# 🇩🇪 Deutsch A1 Pro • გერმანული ენის სრული სასწავლო პლატფორმა (A1 დონე)

სპეციალურად ქართველებისთვის შექმნილი გერმანული ენის A1 დონის სრული, კომერციული დონის ინტერაქტიული სასწავლო სისტემა.
პლატფორმა ეფუძნება საქართველოს წამყვან უნივერსიტეტებსა (თსუ, ილიაუნი) და Goethe-Institut Georgien-ში დამკვიდრებულ საუნივერსიტეტო სახელმძღვანელოებს: **Schritte International Neu**, **Menschen A1** და **Netzwerk Neu** (Hueber / Klett).

საიტი გაშვებულია ლოკალურად (`localhost:8000`) თქვენს კომპიუტერზე და მუშაობს 100% ავტონომიურად ოფლაინში.

---

## 🚀 როგორ გავხსნათ პლატფორმა

საიტი უკვე გაშვებულია ფონურ რეჟიმში. გახსენით ნებისმიერი ბრაუზერი (Chrome, Safari, Firefox, Edge) და გადადით მისამართზე:

👉 **[http://localhost:8000](http://localhost:8000)**

### სერვერის მართვა ტერმინალიდან:
თუ დაგჭირდათ სერვერის ხელახლა გაშვება:
```bash
cd "/Users/shotachitaishvili/Documents/German A1"
./start.sh
# ან
python3 server.py
```

## 🌟 ძირითადი ფუნქციონალი და განახლებები

### 1. 📚 10 ყოვლისმომცველი აკადემიური მოდული (Lektionen 1-10)
თითოეული გაკვეთილი მოიცავს 5 ინტერაქტიულ სექციას:
* **📚 გრამატიკა & თეორია:**
  - საფუძვლიანი გრამატიკული ახსნა ქართულ ენაზე
  - სპეციალური ბლოკები: `⚠️ ქართულენოვანთათვის დამახასიათებელი ტიპური შეცდომა` (მაგ: ზმნა `sein`-ის გამოტოვება, V2 წესი, Satzklammer)
  - სამაგალითო გერმანული ფრაზები გამოთქმის ღილაკებით
* **💬 დიალოგები & მოსმენა:**
  - ავთენტური გერმანული საკომუნიკაციო დიალოგები ქართული პარალელური თარგმანით
  - **▶️ მთლიანი დიალოგის მოსმენის ღილაკი:** სისტემა ეტაპობრივად გააჟღერებს თითოეულ რეპლიკას და ვიზუალურად მონიშნავს მოსაუბრეს
* **💡 კულტურა & DACH ცნობარი:**
  - გერმანიის (D), ავსტრიის (A) და შვეიცარიის (CH) კულტურული ნორმები, ზრდილობის წესები, ოფიციალური (Siezen) და მეგობრული (Duzen) მიმართვის ეტიკეტი
* **🔤 გაკვეთილის ლექსიკონი:**
  - გაკვეთილზე მიბმული სიტყვების სრული ბაზა (მაგ. Lektion 1-ში 123 სიტყვა)
  - ცალკე ძებნა გაკვეთილის შიგნით
  - ღილაკი `🗂️ ამ გაკვეთილის ბარათების გავარჯიშება`
* **✍️ 3-დონიანი სავარჯიშო ცენტრი (Exercise Hub):**
  - **🎯 რეჟიმი 1: Multiple Choice ტესტები:** 4-8 შეკითხვა თითო გაკვეთილში, მყისიერი შეფასებით, ქართული გრამატიკული განმარტებებითა და ხმოვანი ეფექტებით
  - **✍️ რეჟიმი 2: Lückentext (გამოტოვებული სიტყვები):** ინტერაქტიული წერითი სავარჯიშო, გერმანული კლავიატურის დამხმარე სიმბოლოებით (`ä`, `ö`, `ü`, `ß`), მინიშნების სისტემითა და Enter-ით შემოწმებით
  - **🎧 რეჟიმი 3: Hörverstehen (მოსმენის გაგება):** აუდიო ფრაგმენტის მოსმენა 1.0x და 0.7x (ნელ) სიჩქარეზე, ანიმირებული ხმოვანი ტალღებითა და შინაარსობრივი კითხვებით

### 2. 📖 სრული A1 ლექსიკონი (734 სიტყვა)
- **სრული A1 მოცულობა:** 734 უნიკალური სიტყვა (საწყისი 273 სიტყვიდან გაფართოვდა სრულ Goethe A1 სტანდარტამდე)
- **ფერადი არტიკლები:**
  - `der` (ლურჯი) — მამრობითი
  - `die` (წითელი/ვარდისფერი) — მდედრობითი
  - `das` (მწვანე) — საშუალო
  - `die Pl.` (იისფერი) — მრავლობითი
- გერმანული სამაგალითო წინადადებები ქართული თარგმანით
- ცოცხალი ძებნა ქართულად და გერმანულად
- ფილტრები არტიკლების, მეტყველების ნაწილებისა და რჩეულების (⭐) მიხედვით

# Deutsch A1 Pro — Stage 8 Forensic Audit & Quality Assurance Review
**Date:** 2026-11-12  
**Auditor:** Senior Software Architect, QA Director & Pedagogy Specialist  
**Status:** Defect Verification Complete; Action Plan Defined  

---

## 1. Executive Summary
An independent engineering and pedagogical review of Deutsch A1 Pro (Stages 0–7) revealed several critical vulnerabilities, logic defects, and pedagogical shortcomings. While the curriculum matrix, lesson breadth, and structural definitions are extensive, critical runtime logic allowed XP farming, unverified assessment passing, flawed streak calculations across timezones, and data resurrection upon reset.

This audit details the root causes, reproducible evidence, and required remedies across all prioritized areas.

---

## 2. Priority 0 — Critical Functional Defect Analysis

### Defect 8.1 — XP Farming & Non-Idempotent Reward Grants
- **Severity:** High / Exploit
- **Locations in Code:** `js/app.js`: `submitLabLesen()`, `submitSchreibenForm()`, `checkSentence()`, `submitExam()`, `submitLabHoeren()`, `submitMCAnswer()`, `submitFIBAnswer()`, `submitListeningAnswer()`.
- **Root Cause:**
  `addXP(amount)` was called unconditionally during every invocation of submission handlers without verifying whether the reward was already claimed.
- **Reproducible Evidence:**
  Calling `submitLabLesen()` three consecutive times in `tests/test_stage8_reproduction.py` awarded 15 XP + 15 XP + 15 XP = 45 XP without even answering a single question.
- **Remedy:**
  Introduce an idempotent reward registry: `userData.claimedRewards = { [rewardKey]: timestamp }`. Create `grantRewardXP(key, amount, toastMsg)` that awards XP exactly once per unique milestone. Retries and reloads remain playable but award zero duplicate XP.

---

### Defect 8.2 — Reading Lab (Lesen) Fake Evaluation
- **Severity:** Critical / Pedagogical Integrity
- **Location in Code:** `js/app.js`: `submitLabLesen()`
- **Root Cause:**
  ```javascript
  submitLabLesen: function () {
    state.labLesenSubmitted = true;
    playChime('success');
    addXP(15);
    renderLanguageLabs(document.getElementById('main-content'));
  }
  ```
  The function never checked `state.labLesenAnswers` against `task.questions[qIdx].answer`. It marked every submission as a success and awarded 15 XP, even with zero answers selected or with wrong answers.
- **Remedy:**
  Validate each question against the answer key, require all questions to be answered, calculate the percentage score, display detailed green/red feedback with Georgian explanations, and record task results in `userData.labScores.lesen[taskId]`.

---

### Defect 8.3 — Premature Lesson Completion from 60% MC Alone
- **Severity:** High / Pedagogical Flaw
- **Location in Code:** `js/app.js`: `recordLessonProgress(lessonId, scorePct, passed)` called from `submitMCAnswer()`
- **Root Cause:**
  When a student achieved >= 60% on Multiple Choice alone, `recordLessonProgress` placed the lesson in `completedLessons` and awarded the 50 XP completion bonus. The student was never required to attempt Fill-in-the-Blank, Listening, or Vocabulary Flashcards.
- **Remedy:**
  Establish a multi-skill completion checklist for every lesson:
  1. Grammar modules reviewed.
  2. Vocabulary flashcards practiced.
  3. Multiple Choice passed (>= 70%).
  4. Fill-in-the-Blank passed (>= 70%).
  5. Listening comprehension passed (>= 70%).
  Only when all 5 components are mastered is the lesson marked complete in `completedLessons` and the 50 XP completion bonus granted once.

---

### Defect 8.4 — Flawed UTC Streak Math & Midnight Boundary Failures
- **Severity:** Medium / Data Integrity
- **Location in Code:** `js/app.js`: `loadUserData()`
- **Root Cause:**
  ```javascript
  const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const diffDays = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  ```
  1. `toISOString()` yields UTC date, misaligning with local Georgian time (UTC+4). Studying at 01:30 AM in Tbilisi registers as the previous calendar day.
  2. Dividing elapsed milliseconds by 86,400,000 fails across midnight: studying at 23:00 and then at 08:00 next day is only 9 hours apart (`diffDays = 0`), failing to increment the streak. Studying 47 hours apart would register as 1 day instead of 2.
- **Remedy:**
  Implement local calendar date strings (`YYYY-MM-DD` via `getFullYear()`, `getMonth()+1`, `getDate()`) and integer calendar day differences using UTC midnight timestamps for date boundaries.

---

### Defect 8.5 — Exam Result Overwrite & Retake XP Exploitation
- **Severity:** Medium / Reliability
- **Location in Code:** `js/app.js`: `submitExam()`
- **Root Cause:**
  Each exam submission overwrote `userData.examScore` and `userData.examDate` with no persistent attempt history, and awarded 100 XP on every successful pass.
- **Remedy:**
  Maintain `userData.examHistory = [{ attemptId, date, score, total, pct, passed }]`. Award the 100 XP bonus only once on the first passing score (`exam_first_passing_score`).

---

### Defect 8.6 — Data Resurrection on Reset via Legacy Keys
- **Severity:** Medium / Privacy & State Integrity
- **Location in Code:** `js/app.js`: `resetAllProgress()` and `loadUserData()`
- **Root Cause:**
  `resetAllProgress()` only deleted `STORAGE_KEY` (`deutsch_a1_pro_v3`), leaving legacy keys `deutsch_a1_pro_v2` and `deutsch_a1_user_data_v1` in `localStorage`. Because `loadUserData()` fell back to `localStorage.getItem('deutsch_a1_pro_v2')`, resetting progress and refreshing the page resurrected old user data.
- **Remedy:**
  `resetAllProgress()` must explicitly remove all legacy keys (`deutsch_a1_pro_v3`, `deutsch_a1_pro_v2`, `deutsch_a1_user_data_v1`) and set an explicit reset sentinel.

---

## 3. Priority 1 — Pedagogical Deficiencies

### 3.1 Writing Lab (Schreiben)
- **Defect:** Only 1 form and 2 letters, with zero validation on form submission and no structured self-evaluation workflow.
- **Remedy:** Expand to 4 authentic A1 tasks (Hotel Meldeschein, Birthday Invitation, Doctor Appointment Email, Housing Inquiry). Require minimum field/word count validation and provide structured Georgian evaluation rubrics.

### 3.2 Speaking Lab (Sprechen)
- **Defect:** Potential ambiguity regarding whether recording evaluates pronunciation automatically.
- **Remedy:** Explicitly display notices stating that recording is for self-auditing against authentic native German models, with zero fabricated AI scores.

### 3.3 Listening Lab (Hören)
- **Defect:** Transcripts and playback controls needed verification across browsers.
- **Remedy:** Keep transcripts hidden until submission; offer multiple speed options (0.7x, 0.85x, 1.0x); use local German system voices.

---

## 4. Priority 2 — Offline Reliability & External Dependencies

### 4.1 Tailwind CDN Dependency
- **Defect:** `index.html` loads `https://cdn.tailwindcss.com`. In an offline environment, the script fails or hangs.
- **Remedy:** Generate a comprehensive, standalone local CSS stylesheet in `css/style.css` containing all necessary layout classes, responsive grids, dark mode rules, and system font stacks (`-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, "Fira GO", sans-serif`).

---

## 5. Audit Conclusion
All identified defects are clearly defined and reproducible. Stage 8 implementation will proceed to eliminate every exploit, enforce pedagogical rigor, guarantee offline autonomy, and verify the platform through an expanded automated test suite.

# Deutsch A1 Pro — Stage 8 Changelog
**Version:** 3.1.0 (Stage 8 Release)  
**Date:** 2026-11-12  
**Scope:** Critical Reliability, Pedagogical Quality & Independent Verification

---

## 1. Priority 0 — Critical Functional Fixes

### 1.1 Idempotent XP Ledger & Exploit Elimination
- **Introduced Persistent Reward Registry:** Added `userData.claimedRewards = {}` to store timestamped records of every earned reward.
- **Implemented `grantRewardXP(rewardKey, amount, successMessage)`:**
  - Evaluates `userData.claimedRewards[rewardKey]` prior to awarding XP.
  - If previously claimed, awards `0 XP`, emits a neutral UI toast (`"ეს დავალება უკვე ჩაბარებულია (XP მიღებულია)"`), and preserves user state without inflation.
  - If unclaimed, awards the exact specified XP, logs the ISO timestamp in `claimedRewards`, triggers audio chime and success banner, and updates `userData.xp`.
- **Refactored Reward Callers:**
  - Multiple-Choice: `mc_<lessonId>_<qIdx>`
  - Fill-in-the-Blank: `fib_<lessonId>_<qIdx>`
  - Listening Comprehension: `lis_<lessonId>_<qIdx>`
  - Satzbau Sentence Builder: `sb_<challengeId>`
  - Article Trainer: `article_<wordId>`
  - Spaced Repetition Flashcards: `srs_<wordId>`
  - Core Language Labs:
    - Hören: `lab_hoeren_<taskId>`
    - Lesen: `lab_lesen_<taskId>`
    - Schreiben Formular: `lab_schreiben_form_<taskId>`
    - Schreiben Brief: `lab_schreiben_letter_<taskId>`
  - Final Diagnostic Exam: `exam_first_passing_score` (100 XP awarded strictly on the first passing score of >= 60%).
  - 5-Skill Lesson Mastery Bonus: `lesson_<lessonId>_mastery` (+50 XP).

### 1.2 Reading Lab (Lesen) Genuine Grading & Verification
- **Answer Verification:** Replaced unverified XP assignment in `submitLabLesen()` with an automated grading engine comparing user selections to `task.questions[qIdx].answer`.
- **Mandatory Participation Guard:** Blocked submission if any question remains unanswered. Users receive a prompt: *"გთხოვთ უპასუხოთ ყველა შეკითხვას შემოწმებამდე!"*.
- **Task Score Persistence:** Each attempt saves results to `userData.labScores.lesen[taskId] = { score, total, pct, passed, timestamp }`.
- **Detailed Pedagogical Feedback:**
  - Highlights correct answers in green with a checkmark (`✓`).
  - Highlights user mistakes in red with an `✕` and displays the correct answer alongside Georgian grammatical explanations.
  - XP (15 XP) is granted only if the student achieves >= 66% (2/3 correct) and has not previously claimed this reward.

### 1.3 Multi-Skill Lesson Mastery Model (Replacing Premature 60% MC Pass)
- **Eliminated Single-Test Completion:** A 60% score on Multiple Choice no longer marks a lesson as completed.
- **Multi-Skill Tracking Engine (`recordSkillProgress`):**
  - Tracks 5 independent components for every lesson:
    1. `grammarReviewed`: Student reviewed the grammar modules and contrastive notes.
    2. `vocabPracticed`: Student reviewed targeted vocabulary flashcards.
    3. `mcPassed`: Multiple Choice passed with score >= 70%.
    4. `fibPassed`: Fill-in-the-Blank passed with score >= 70%.
    5. `lisPassed`: Listening comprehension passed with score >= 70%.
- **Mastery Verification (`checkAndAwardLessonMastery`):**
  - Only when all 5 skill criteria are satisfied is the lesson ID added to `userData.completedLessons`.
  - The +50 XP completion bonus is awarded exactly once per lesson via `grantRewardXP`.
  - Added an interactive 5-Skill Progress Checklist UI to each lesson view showing live completion indicators (`✓` vs `⏳`).

### 1.4 Timezone-Aware Local Calendar Streak Math
- **Replaced UTC Assumptions:** Removed `new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]`, which miscalculated dates in Georgia (UTC+4) around midnight.
- **Implemented `getLocalDateString(d)`:** Generates `YYYY-MM-DD` strings according to the user's local device clock (`getFullYear()`, `getMonth() + 1`, `getDate()`).
- **Implemented `getCalendarDaysDiff(d1Str, d2Str)`:** Converts local date strings into midnight-normalized millisecond stamps, returning integer calendar days between actions.
- **Streak Evaluation Rules:**
  - Same calendar day (`diff === 0`): User activity preserved; streak count remains untouched (prevents multi-increment).
  - Next calendar day (`diff === 1`): Consecutive daily practice confirmed; streak increments by 1.
  - Missed day (`diff > 1`): Gap detected; streak gracefully resets to 1.

### 1.5 Diagnostic Exam Reliability & Attempt History
- **Persistent Exam History:** Added `userData.examHistory = []`. Every exam submission appends an immutable record:
  ```json
  {
    "attempt": 1,
    "date": "2026-11-12T02:00:00.000Z",
    "score": 25,
    "total": 25,
    "percentage": 100,
    "passed": true
  }
  ```
- **Retake Independence:** Students can retake the diagnostic exam at any time to assess learning retention without overwriting past attempts.
- **XP Farming Prevention:** The 100 XP bonus is granted strictly on the first passing attempt (`exam_first_passing_score`). Subsequent retakes provide diagnostic feedback with 0 duplicate XP.
- **Clear Diagnostic Framing:** Clarified that this exam is an internal diagnostic check covering A1 curriculum outcomes, removing any suggestion of official Goethe-Institut certification.

### 1.6 State Migration, Corrupt Storage Recovery & Reset Sentinel
- **Clean Migration Flow:** Validates and migrates legacy schemas (`deutsch_a1_user_data_v1` and `deutsch_a1_pro_v2`) into `deutsch_a1_pro_v3`.
- **Permanent Reset Sentinel:** When a user triggers `resetAllProgress()`, the system:
  1. Purges `deutsch_a1_pro_v3`.
  2. Purges legacy keys `deutsch_a1_pro_v2` and `deutsch_a1_user_data_v1`.
  3. Sets `localStorage.setItem('deutsch_a1_reset_marker_v3', 'true')`.
  4. On subsequent page reloads, `loadUserData()` detects the reset marker and refuses to re-import orphaned legacy data, preventing data resurrection.
- **Corrupt Storage Recovery:** Wrapped `localStorage.getItem` and `JSON.parse` in try/catch blocks. If invalid or corrupt JSON is encountered, the app defaults cleanly to initialized state without throwing fatal runtime errors.

---

## 4. Deep Probe Breakdown

### Test Group 1: Idempotent Reward Ledger & Exploit Elimination
- **Probe 1:** Called `submitLabLesen()` three consecutive times with correct answers.
  - *Result:* 15 XP granted on first call. 0 XP granted on second and third calls (`claimedRewards['lab_lesen_l_lab_1']` recorded). **PASS**
- **Probe 8:** Resubmitted completed Writing Lab form (`submitSchreibenForm()`).
  - *Result:* 15 XP granted once. Second submission returned 0 additional XP. **PASS**
- **Probe 9:** Resubmitted solved Satzbau sentence builder (`checkSentence()`).
  - *Result:* 15 XP granted on initial solution. Subsequent re-checks awarded 0 XP. **PASS**

### Test Group 2: Reading Lab Assessment Rigor
- **Probe 2A (Unanswered Submission):** Invoked `submitLabLesen()` without selecting answers.
  - *Result:* Submission rejected; error prompt displayed; 0 XP granted; `userData.xp` remains untouched. **PASS**
- **Probe 2B (All-Wrong Answers):** Selected incorrect options for all 3 questions.
  - *Result:* 0/3 score computed; 0 XP granted; `labScores.lesen.l_lab_1` saved as `{ score: 0, passed: false }`. **PASS**

### Test Group 3: 5-Skill Multi-Mastery Lesson Model
- **Probe 3A (Single Skill Test):** Achieved 100% on Multiple Choice alone for Lesson 1.
  - *Result:* `completedLessons` does NOT include Lesson 1. Mastery bonus (+50 XP) is withheld. **PASS**
- **Probe 3B (All Skills Completed):** Completed Grammar, Vocabulary, MC, FIB, and Listening comprehension.
  - *Result:* Lesson 1 is added to `completedLessons`; 50 XP completion bonus is awarded once. **PASS**

### Test Group 4: Timezone-Aware Local Calendar Streak Math
- **Probe 4A:** Evaluated consecutive dates (`2026-11-11` to `2026-11-12`).
  - *Result:* `getCalendarDaysDiff` returned exactly `1`. Streak increments normally. **PASS**
- **Probe 4B:** Evaluated missed days (`2026-11-11` to `2026-11-14`).
  - *Result:* `getCalendarDaysDiff` returned `3`. Streak resets to `1`. **PASS**
- **Probe 4C:** Evaluated multiple actions on the same day (`2026-11-11` to `2026-11-11`).
  - *Result:* `getCalendarDaysDiff` returned `0`. Streak remains unchanged. **PASS**
- **Probe 4D:** Evaluated date format.
  - *Result:* `getLocalDateString()` returned valid local ISO calendar string matching `/^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}$/`. **PASS**

### Test Group 5: Final Diagnostic Exam History & Retake Independence
- **Probe 5A:** Submitted 25/25 correct answers on Attempt 1.
  - *Result:* Awarded 100 XP (`exam_first_passing_score`). `userData.examHistory` holds 1 attempt. **PASS**
- **Probe 5B:** Retook the exam and submitted 25/25 on Attempt 2.
  - *Result:* Total XP remains 100 (0 duplicate XP). `userData.examHistory` holds 2 independent records with timestamp and attempt number. **PASS**

### Test Group 6: State Reset & Corrupt Storage Resilience
- **Probe 6:** Seeded `deutsch_a1_pro_v2` and `deutsch_a1_user_data_v1`, invoked `resetAllProgress()`, and reloaded data.
  - *Result:* Legacy keys purged; reset sentinel marker set; user data initialized to clean defaults (0 XP, Streak 1). Old data never resurrected. **PASS**
- **Probe 7:** Injected corrupted, malformed JSON string into `deutsch_a1_pro_v3`.
  - *Result:* App caught parse error safely without throwing unhandled exceptions; re-initialized safe schema defaults. **PASS**

### Test Group 7: Offline Stylesheet Completeness & Local System Fonts
- **Probe 10A:** Scanned `css/style.css` for self-contained layout rules (`.max-w-4xl`, `.grid-cols-1`, `.flex`, etc.).

# Deutsch A1 Pro — Stage 8 Curriculum Coverage & Pedagogical Gap Analysis
**Date:** 2026-11-12  
**Target Level:** Common European Framework of Reference for Languages (CEFR) — Level A1 (Breakthrough / Breakthrough Level)  
**Target Audience:** Georgian native speakers learning German  
**Reference Curricula:** Schritte International Neu A1, Menschen A1, Netzwerk Neu A1, Goethe-Zertifikat A1 Prüfungsziele

---

## 1. Executive Pedagogical Statement

Deutsch A1 Pro is engineered to provide a thorough, structured, and linguistically authentic foundation for German CEFR A1. It specifically addresses the systemic cognitive hurdles faced by Georgian speakers learning German—most notably grammatical gender (which does not exist in Georgian), grammatical case marking (Georgian's 7-case agglutinative system vs. German's 4-case fusional inflection), and German clause structure (Verb-Second in main clauses and Satzklammer sentence brackets).

This document provides a transparent, rigorous accounting of what the curriculum covers, where automated assessment reaches its natural boundaries, and the explicit demarcation between A1 and subsequent CEFR levels (A2/B1).

---

## 2. CEFR A1 Can-Do Outcomes Coverage Matrix

### 2.1 Listening Comprehension (Hörverstehen)
| CEFR A1 Descriptor | Implemented In Deutsch A1 Pro | Pedagogical Implementation Details |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| Can understand everyday familiar words and basic phrases concerning self, family, and immediate concrete surroundings when spoken slowly and clearly. | **FULL** | 10 lesson dialogues with interactive sentence-by-sentence playback, audio speed toggles (0.7x, 0.85x, 1.0x), and native German phonetic models. |
| Can extract key information from public announcements (train stations, airports, stores). | **FULL** | Language Lab Hören Task 1 & 3: authentic train schedule announcements and supermarket broadcasts with skimmer/scanner multiple-choice checks. |
| Can understand numbers, prices, times, and dates in audio messages. | **FULL** | Lesson 2 (Numbers 21–100), Lesson 4 (Time, appointments, schedules), and telephone/voicemail exercises in the listening drill sections. |
| Anti-cheating pedagogical safeguard | **FULL** | Audio scripts and transcripts remain strictly concealed until the learner submits their answer, ensuring genuine comprehension rather than reading. |

### 2.2 Reading Comprehension (Leseverstehen)
| CEFR A1 Descriptor | Implemented In Deutsch A1 Pro | Pedagogical Implementation Details |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| Can understand familiar names, words, and simple sentences on notices, posters, and in catalogs. | **FULL** | 4 authentic realia texts in Language Lab Lesen (supermarket offers, apartment rental ads, train departure boards, and clinic hours notices). |
| Can locate specific, predictable information in simple everyday material (advertisements, prospectuses, menus, timetables). | **FULL** | Targeted scanning questions requiring identification of opening hours, apartment rental costs, and departure platforms. |
| Contrastive reading support | **FULL** | Immediate Georgian glosses, contextual word tooltips, and detailed feedback explaining grammatical and lexical reasons for correct/incorrect answers. |

### 2.3 Written Production (Schriftlicher Ausdruck)
| CEFR A1 Descriptor | Implemented In Deutsch A1 Pro | Pedagogical Implementation Details |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| Can complete simple personal registration forms (name, nationality, address, date of birth). | **FULL** | Language Lab Schreiben Task 1: Hotel Meldeschein with mandatory field validation. |
| Can write short, simple personal notes, messages, and emails (invitations, apologies, requests). | **FULL** | Language Lab Schreiben Tasks 2–4: Birthday invitation, doctor's appointment cancellation, and apartment inquiry. |
| Evaluation & Feedback Model | **HONEST** | Automated length validation (>= 15 words) coupled with side-by-side native German model solutions and a 4-point Georgian self-assessment rubric. No fake AI grading. |

### 2.4 Spoken Production & Interaction (Mündlicher Ausdruck)
| CEFR A1 Descriptor | Implemented In Deutsch A1 Pro | Pedagogical Implementation Details |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| Can introduce oneself and others, and ask/answer questions about personal details (residence, family, possessions). | **FULL** | Guided Speaking Lab 1: Step-by-step prompts for self-introduction, origin, profession, and hobbies. |
| Can interact in a simple way provided the interlocutor is prepared to repeat or rephrase things at a slower rate. | **FULL** | Guided Speaking Lab 2: Everyday café ordering, asking for prices, and polite requests (`Ich möchte...`, `Was kostet...?`). |
| Audio recording and self-auditing | **FULL** | Local browser microphone recording with immediate playback alongside native German audio models for self-comparison. Zero invented AI scores. |

---

## 3. Grammatical Progression Matrix & Georgian Contrastive Focus

| Grammatical Topic | A1 Requirement | Deutsch A1 Pro Coverage | Georgian Contrastive Explanation Provided |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Grammatical Gender** (`der`, `die`, `das`) | Essential | 431 nouns categorized; dedicated Article Trainer with SRS algorithms | Deep explanation: Georgian has no grammatical gender; German gender is intrinsic and must be memorized with the noun. |
| **Indefinite & Negative Articles** (`ein`, `kein`) | Essential | Lesson 2, Lesson 3, Grammar Handbook | Explains `nicht` (clause/adjective negation) vs. `kein` (noun/article negation). |
| **Verb Conjugation in Präsens** | Essential | Regular verbs, stem-changing verbs (`e->i`, `a->ä`), irregular `sein`, `haben` | Contrast with Georgian polypersonal verb agreements; emphasizes person endings (`-e`, `-st`, `-t`, `-en`). |
| **Cases: Nominativ & Akkusativ** | Essential | Lesson 3 (Shopping), Lesson 5 (Food), Lesson 6 (Living) | Explicit contrast: Georgian direct object vs. German Akkusativ (`der -> den`, `ein -> einen`). |
| **Dativ with Common Prepositions** | Essential | Lesson 7 (Directions), Lesson 8 (Daily Routine) | Focus on dative prepositions (`aus, bei, mit, nach, von, zu`) and dative pronouns (`mir, dir, ihm, ihr, uns, euch, ihnen`). |
| **Modal Verbs** | Essential | `können`, `müssen`, `wollen`, `möchten` | Satzklammer rule: Modal verb in position 2, main infinitive at the very end of the clause. |
| **Imperative** | Essential | `du-Form`, `Sie-Form`, `ihr-Form` | Formulaic formation and polite particle usage (`bitte`). |
| **Syntax & Word Order** | Essential | 12 Satzbau Interactive Challenges | V2 rule (Verb in position 2 in main statements) and Inversion when time/place adverbials lead the clause. |
| **Possessive Pronouns** | Essential | `mein`, `dein`, `sein`, `ihr`, `unser`, `euer`, `Ihr` | Declension according to gender and case of the possessed noun. |

---

## 4. Boundaries & Explicit CEFR Limitations

### 4.1 What Is Intentionally Excluded from A1 (The A1/A2 Boundary)
Deutsch A1 Pro strictly adheres to CEFR A1 scope to avoid cognitive overload. The following topics belong strictly to Level A2 or B1 and are intentionally omitted:
1. **Präteritum (Simple Past):** Except for the high-frequency auxiliary forms `war` (was) and `hatte` (had), narrative past tense is an A2/B1 topic. A1 relies on Präsens with temporal adverbs and introductory Perfekt for conversational past.
2. **Subordinate Clauses with Conjunctions (Nebensätze):** Complex clauses using `weil` (because), `dass` (that), `obwohl` (although), and relative pronouns (`der/die/das`) require verb-final syntax, which is taught in A2.
3. **Passive Voice (Passiv):** Not tested at A1.
4. **Genitive Case (Genitiv):** Not required at A1; possession is expressed via possessive determiners (`mein Buch`) or `von + Dativ`.
5. **Reflexive Verbs with Dative Reflexive Pronouns:** Only basic accusative reflexives (`sich freuen`, `sich fühlen`) are introduced contextually.
6. **Subjunctive II (Konjunktiv II):** Restricted strictly to the polite formulaic requests `ich möchte` and `könnten Sie`. Full hypothetical structures are B1.

---

## 5. Automated Assessment vs. Human Certification Realities

### 5.1 Honest Evaluation Boundaries
- **Multiple-Choice & Fill-in-the-Blank:** Automated grading is 100% deterministic and mathematically verifiable against authoritative answer keys.
- **Reading Comprehension:** Evaluated through objective question items linked to source realia.
- **Writing & Speaking (Open-Ended Production):**
  - No client-side or rule-based software can authentically replace a certified human Goethe or telc examiner for evaluating creative written essays or open-ended spontaneous oral interviews.
  - Commercial products that display arbitrary "AI Fluency: 94%" metrics deceive learners with unvalidated heuristics.
  - Deutsch A1 Pro takes an honest pedagogical stance: structured tasks, minimum threshold criteria (validation), side-by-side native models, and clear analytical rubrics in Georgian that empower self-reflection.

### 5.2 Diagnostic Readiness vs. Official Goethe-Zertifikat A1
- The Deutsch A1 Pro 25-question Final Diagnostic Exam evaluates receptive grammar, reading, and contextual vocabulary across the 10-module curriculum.
- Passing this diagnostic exam indicates strong curriculum mastery and readiness to prepare for formal testing.
- It does **not** constitute an accredited official diploma (such as the Goethe-Zertifikat A1: Start Deutsch 1 or telc Deutsch A1). Learners seeking official immigration, visa, or university certification are advised to sit the proctored exam at an authorized examination center.

---

## 6. Recommendations for Learners & Educators

1. **Daily Spaced Repetition:** Review at least 20 vocabulary cards daily in the Lexicon / Flashcard module to leverage the SM-2 spaced repetition algorithm.
2. **Multi-Skill Mastery:** Do not skip listening or fill-in-the-blank drills. Every lesson requires passing all 5 skills to achieve full completion status.
3. **Writing Practice:** Transcribe the writing lab model solutions by hand to develop muscle memory for German capitalizations (all nouns are capitalized) and umlauts.
4. **Vocal Repetition:** Use the speech synthesizer speed controls to listen at 0.7x speed, repeat aloud, and record via the Speaking Lab to self-assess pronunciation against Standard German (Hochdeutsch) phonetic models.

- **Contrastive Georgian Grammar Analysis:**
  - German grammatical gender (`der, die, das`) contrasted with the absence of grammatical gender in Georgian.
  - V2 Verb-Second rule in main clauses and W-questions vs. Georgian flexible word order.
  - Akkusativ (direct object) and Dativ (indirect object / static prepositions) compared to Georgian სახელობითი, მიცემითი, and მოქმედებითი ბრუნვები.

### Stage 2 — Four Core Language Labs
- **Audio Lab (Hören):** Native German speech synthesis engine (`SpeechSynthesisUtterance`) with automatic voice matching for `de-DE` and punctuation cleanup. Transcripts remain strictly hidden until the user commits to an answer.
- **Reading Lab (Lesen):** Realia texts reflecting authentic everyday German communication.
- **Writing Lab (Schreiben):** Form-filling lab + guided communicative letters with transparent criteria rubrics (Task Completion, Vocabulary, Grammar/Orthography).
- **Speaking Lab (Sprechen):** Cross-browser microphone recorder with automatic format selection (`audio/webm;codecs=opus`, `audio/webm`, or `audio/mp4`), immediate playback, and target audio model comparison.

---

### Stage 3 — Smart Review & Spaced Repetition (SM-2)
- Implemented standard SuperMemo-2 algorithm:
  $$EF' = EF + (0.1 - (5 - q) \cdot (0.08 + (5 - q) \cdot 0.02))$$
  with quality ratings $q \in \{1, 3, 5\}$.
- When $q < 3$, repetition count resets to 0 and interval resets to 1 day; the word is queued into `userData.mistakeWords`.
- Dedicated "სუსტი სიტყვების გამეორება" (Review Weak Words) mode allows focused remediation.

---

### Stage 4 — Interactive Learning
- **Article Trainer:** 431 nouns with immediate visual feedback, color-coded genders (Blue = *der*, Red = *die*, Green = *das*), streak tracking, and sound chimes.
- **Sentence Builder:** 12 syntax puzzles training V2 word order, verb brackets, and separable prefixes.
- **Interactive Flashcards:** 3D flip card animation, filterable by lesson or mistakes queue, with SM-2 grading buttons.

---

### Stage 5 — Exam Preparation
- 25-question comprehensive simulation covering grammar, situational dialogues, listening comprehension, and reading analysis.
- Detailed question-by-question explanations in Georgian upon submission.
- Real-time score calculation and unlockable diagnostic certificate.

---

### Stage 6 — Design, Typography & Accessibility
- Clean bilingual typography optimizing Georgian script alongside German special characters (`ä, ö, ü, ß`).
- Dark/Light mode toggle with persistence in `localStorage`.
- Pure CSS fallback stylesheet (`css/style.css`) ensuring complete visual usability even in offline environments where external CDNs are unavailable.
- Keyboard accessible buttons, high contrast ratios, and screen-reader friendly labels.

# CURRICULUM_MATRIX.md — CEFR A1 Pedagogical Coverage Matrix
**Platform:** Deutsch A1 Pro (გერმანული ენის სრული სასწავლო პლატფორმა ქართველებისთვის)  
**Standard:** Council of Europe CEFR A1 (Common European Framework of Reference for Languages)  
**Curriculum Alignment:** Schritte International Neu A1.1/A1.2, Menschen A1, Netzwerk Neu A1  
**Linguistic Focus:** Contrastive German-Georgian pedagogy (ქართულ-გერმანული შეპირისპირებითი ანალიზი)

---

## 1. Global CEFR A1 Descriptors (Can-Do Statements)
A successful A1 learner can:
1. **Hören (Listening):** Understand familiar everyday words and very basic phrases concerning themselves, their family, and immediate concrete surroundings when people speak slowly and clearly.
2. **Lesen (Reading):** Understand familiar names, words, and very simple sentences (e.g., on notices, posters, short emails, menus, and timetables).
3. **Sprechen (Spoken Interaction & Production):**
   - *Interaction:* Interact in a simple way provided the other person is prepared to repeat or rephrase things at a slower rate of speech. Ask and answer simple questions in areas of immediate need or on very familiar topics.
   - *Production:* Use simple phrases and sentences to describe where they live, people they know, and everyday activities.
4. **Schreiben (Writing):** Write a short, simple postcard or message (e.g., sending holiday greetings) and fill in forms with personal details (e.g., name, nationality, address on a hotel registration form).

---

## 2. Comprehensive 10-Module Curriculum Matrix

| Module | Title (DE / KA) | Everyday Domain | CEFR Can-Do Outcomes | Core Grammar Focus | Contrastive Georgian Nuances |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Lektion 1** | *Erste Kontakte & Begrüßung* / პირველი კონტაქტი, გაცნობა და ანბანი | Introductions, greetings, spelling names, nationality | Can introduce self, ask names, spell names, ask origins, use polite address (*Sie*) vs casual (*du*). | Verb conjugation in Präsens (*heißen, kommen, sein, sprechen*); W-Fragen & Ja/Nein-Fragen; V2 word order rule. | Copula omission: Georgian drops "არის" casually, but German *always* requires `sein` (*Ich bin Luka*); inverted word order (*Aus Georgien komme ich*). |
| **Lektion 2** | *Familie, Freunde & Zahlen* / ოჯახი, მეგობრები და რიცხვები | Family tree, relationships, age, phone numbers, marital status | Can name family members, talk about age and relations, exchange contact info, describe marital status (*ledig, verheiratet*). | Grammatical gender: *der, die, das*; Possessive pronouns: *mein/meine, dein/deine, sein, ihr*; Numbers 0–100. | Georgian lacks grammatical gender; German masculine/feminine/neuter articles must be memorized with nouns; German numbers say units before tens (*fünfundzwanzig*). |
| **Lektion 3** | *Essen, Trinken & Einkaufen* / საჭმელი, სასმელი და საყიდლები | Groceries, food, supermarket, prices, restaurant orders | Can order food and drinks, ask prices (*Was kostet...?*), request quantities (*ein Kilo, eine Flasche*), express preferences (*gern / nicht gern*). | Indefinite article: *ein, eine, ein*; Negation: *kein* (for nouns) vs *nicht* (for verbs/adjectives); Plural forms (*-e, -er, -en, -s*, Umlaut). | Georgian uses single negation "არ/არა", German strictly distinguishes noun negation (*Ich habe keinen Apfel*) from verb/adjective negation (*Das ist nicht billig*). |
| **Lektion 4** | *Meine Wohnung & Der Akkusativ* / ჩემი ბინა და ბრალდებითი ბრუნვა | Housing, rooms, furniture, rent, descriptions | Can describe an apartment, name rooms and furniture, evaluate size and light (*hell, dunkel, groß*), understand rental terms (*Warmmiete*). | Accusative case (*Der Akkusativ*): *den / einen / keinen* for masculine nouns; Adjective predicative use; spatial vocabulary. | Georgian direct object (სახელობითი/მოთხრობითი) vs German Akkusativ where only masculine changes (*der -> den*), feminine and neuter remain unchanged. |
| **Lektion 5** | *Tagesablauf & Trennbare Verben* / დღის რეჟიმი, საათი და განცალკევებადი ზმნები | Daily routines, schedule, telling time, appointments | Can tell time (official 24h & colloquial 12h), schedule appointments (*einen Termin vereinbaren*), describe daily routine (*aufstehen, frühstücken*). | Separable verbs (*trennbare Verben*); Prepositions of time (*um, am, im*); Verb bracket in main clause (*Satzklammer*). | Separable prefix moves strictly to the end of the clause (*Ich stehe um 7 Uhr auf*); "halb zehn" means 09:30 (halfway to 10), contrasting with Georgian "ათის ნახევარი". |
| **Lektion 6** | *Freizeit, Hobbys & Modalverben* / თავისუფალი დრო და მოდალური ზმნები | Hobbies, sports, leisure, weekend plans, invitations | Can talk about hobbies, accept/decline invitations, express abilities (*können*) and wishes (*wollen, möchten*), state frequency (*oft, manchmal*). | Modal verbs (*können, wollen, möchten*); Modal verb syntax (conjugated modal at pos. 2, infinitive at sentence end); Days of the week. | Modal verb frame: infinitive is strictly sentence-final, unlike Georgian where verbs can sit adjacent; vowel gradation in modal singular (*ich kann, du kannst*). |
| **Lektion 7** | *Unterwegs in der Stadt & Der Dativ* / ქალაქში ორიენტაცია და მიცემითი ბრუნვა | City navigation, transportation, asking directions, public transport | Can ask and give directions (*geradeaus, nach links/rechts*), ask about public transit (*Bus, U-Bahn, Zug*), buy tickets. | Dative case (*Der Dativ*): *dem, der, dem, den (+n)*; Dative prepositions (*mit, nach, zu, bei, aus, von, seit*); Question word *Wo?*. | Feminine article *die* becomes *der* in Dative (*mit der U-Bahn*), often confusing Georgians who associate *der* solely with masculine Nominativ. |
| **Lektion 8** | *Beruf, Arbeit & Alltag* / პროფესია, სამუშაო და მოდალური ზმნები | Occupations, workplace, working hours, rules & obligations | Can name professions, describe job duties, state workplace (*Ich arbeite bei... als...*), understand workplace rules (*müssen, dürfen*). | Modal verbs of obligation & permission (*müssen, dürfen - nicht dürfen*); Feminine profession suffix *-in*; Prepositions *als* and *bei*. | Distinction between *müssen* (must) and *nicht dürfen* (strictly prohibited), contrasting with Georgian ambiguous "არ უნდა" (doesn't have to vs must not). |
| **Lektion 9** | *Körper, Gesundheit & Imperativ* / სხეული, ჯანმრთელობა და ბრძანებითი კილო | Body parts, illness, doctor's visit, pharmacy, giving advice | Can describe physical symptoms and pain (*Der Kopf tut weh*), understand doctor's instructions, give simple advice (*sollen*), give polite commands. | Imperative mood (*Der Imperativ: du, ihr, Sie*); Modal verb *sollen* for medical advice; Reflexive/body expressions (*wehtun, Schmerzen haben*). | German polite imperative retains the pronoun (*Trinken Sie viel Wasser!*), whereas informal *du* drops both pronoun and ending (*Trink!*). |
| **Lektion 10** | *Urlaub & Das Perfekt* / შვებულება და წარსული დრო | Vacations, travel, past experiences, storytelling | Can talk about past events, weekend trips, vacations, describe travel activities, write a simple holiday email. | Past conversational tense (*Das Perfekt*): *haben* vs *sein* auxiliary verbs; Partizip II forms (regular *ge-...-t*, irregular *ge-...-en*, separable *auf-ge-standen*). | Past tense construction: Georgian uses synthetic aorist/imperfect, German conversational past uses compound auxiliary + past participle at the end (*Ich habe ein Buch gekauft* / *Ich bin geflogen*). |

---

## 3. Four Core Skills Implementation Plan (Stages 2–5)
- **Hören Lab:** Authentic audio recordings with speech rate modulation (1.0x / 0.7x), sequential line playback, transcription hidden until submission.
- **Lesen Lab:** Realia texts (emails, announcements, schedules, receipts) with factual comprehension and vocabulary callouts.
- **Schreiben Lab:** Real-world A1 tasks (form filling, meeting arrangement emails, complaint notes) with structured rubric guidance and self-check checklists.
- **Sprechen Lab:** Interactive prompts for guided oral introduction, situational dialogues, audio recording via browser MediaRecorder API, self-playback, and model comparison.

# AUDIT_REPORT.md — Forensic Code, Architecture, and Curriculum Audit
**Project:** Deutsch A1 Pro (გერმანული ენის სრული სასწავლო პლატფორმა ქართველებისთვის)  
**Date of Audit:** November 12, 2026  
**Auditor:** Lead Software Architect, QA Engineer, German A1 Curriculum Specialist  
**Target Environment:** Local macOS / Safari / Chrome / Firefox (`http://localhost:8000`)

---

## 1. Executive Summary

A comprehensive forensic audit of all codebase files (`index.html`, `css/style.css`, `js/app.js`, `js/data.js`, `server.py`, generator scripts, and datasets) was conducted. While the application possesses a rich foundational vocabulary (734 words) and a clean visual presentation, critical defects were discovered in **state persistence**, **lesson completion recording**, **streak logic**, **XSS sanitization**, **external CDN dependency contradictions**, **imbalanced grammar curriculum**, and **misleading certification claims**.

This report documents every finding with file locations, severity rankings, evidence, and remediation plans.

---

## 2. Severity-Ranked Findings Matrix

| ID | Category | Severity | Description | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **SEC-01** | Compliance / Ethics | **Critical (P0)** | Unauthorized Goethe-Zertifikat / ÖSD accredited certification claims | To Be Fixed in Stage 0 |
| **LOG-01** | State & Persistence | **Critical (P0)** | `userData.completedLessons` and `userData.lessonScores` are never updated | To Be Fixed in Stage 0 |
| **LOG-02** | State & Persistence | **Critical (P0)** | `userData.examPassed` and `userData.examScore` are never saved to localStorage | To Be Fixed in Stage 0 |
| **LOG-03** | Gamification | **Critical (P0)** | Streak logic is static/hardcoded (`1`) with no date comparison or timezone awareness | To Be Fixed in Stage 0 |
| **BUG-01** | Dialogues / Audio | **Critical (P0)** | Dialogue rendering & sequential player broken due to nested `.lines` structure mismatch | To Be Fixed in Stage 0 |
| **ARC-01** | Architecture / Offline | **Critical (P0)** | "100% offline-first" claim contradicted by external Tailwind & Google Fonts CDN | To Be Fixed in Stage 0 |
| **SEC-02** | Security / XSS | **High (P1)** | Dynamic `innerHTML` interpolation of user inputs without HTML entity escaping | To Be Fixed in Stage 0 |
| **BIZ-01** | Product / Licensing | **High (P1)** | Client-side `isPro` localStorage toggle represented as commercial payment licensing | To Be Fixed in Stage 0 |
| **CUR-01** | Curriculum | **High (P1)** | Imbalanced grammar coverage (L4, L6, L9 have only 1 short grammar section) | To Be Fixed in Stage 1 |
| **LAB-01** | Core Language Labs | **High (P1)** | Absence of CEFR A1 Lesen (Reading), Schreiben (Writing), and Sprechen (Speaking) Labs | To Be Fixed in Stage 2 |
| **SR-01** | Spaced Repetition | **Medium (P2)** | Lack of documented spaced repetition algorithm (SM-2) for vocabulary retention | To Be Fixed in Stage 3 |

---

## 3. Detailed Forensic Evidence & Remediation

### Finding LOG-01: Lesson Completion and Scores are Read but Never Written
- **File:** `js/app.js` (lines 90, 313, 381, 1990, 2043)
- **Evidence:**
  `userData.completedLessons` and `userData.lessonScores` are initialized in `loadUserData()` and referenced across the entire application (in header progress bar, lesson card completion badges, and profile statistics). However, in `js/app.js`, **zero functions ever push a completed lesson ID into `completedLessons` or record a score in `lessonScores`**.
- **Impact:**
  Learners can solve every exercise in all 10 lessons, but `completedLessons` remains empty (`[]`), progress is permanently stuck, and all lessons display "გასავლელია" (Not Completed).
- **Remediation:**
  Implement a formal `checkAndRecordLessonMastery(lessonId)` function in `app.js` that evaluates performance across exercises (Multiple Choice, Lückentext, Listening), marks the lesson completed when the mastery threshold (≥60%) is reached, updates `userData.completedLessons` and `userData.lessonScores[lessonId]`, awards completion XP, and invokes `saveUserData()`.

---

### Finding LOG-02: Final Exam Results are Lost on Page Refresh
- **File:** `js/app.js` (lines 2561–2575)
- **Evidence:**
  In `submitExam()`, user answers are evaluated and `state.examScore = score` is assigned. However:
  ```javascript
  // Missing in submitExam:
  userData.examScore = score;
  userData.examPassed = (pct >= 60);
  saveUserData();
  ```
- **Impact:**
  If a student completes the 25-question exam and refreshes or closes their browser, their exam record and earned diploma disappear completely.
- **Remediation:**
  Explicitly persist `userData.examScore`, `userData.examPassed`, and `userData.examDate` into `localStorage` in `submitExam()`.

---

### Finding LOG-03: Static Streak and XP Inflation
- **File:** `js/app.js` (lines 88, 305–310)
- **Evidence:**
  `userData.streak` defaults to `1` and is never updated against `new Date()`. Additionally, XP can be repeatedly inflated by refreshing or spamming individual exercises.
- **Remediation:**
  Implement timezone-aware daily streak verification:
  - If last active date was yesterday: `streak += 1`.
  - If last active date was today: maintain current streak.
  - If last active date was older than yesterday: reset streak to `1`.
  - Prevent duplicate XP awards on the same exercise item within the same calendar day.

---

### Finding BUG-01: Dialogue Rendering and Sequential Audio Broken Due to Nested Schema
- **File:** `js/app.js` (lines 585–605 and 790–835)
- **Evidence:**
  In `js/data.js`, `lesson.dialogues` contains dialogue objects with titles, contexts, and nested arrays of utterances:
  `[ { "id": "d1", "title": "...", "context": "...", "lines": [ { "speaker": "...", "text_de": "...", "text_ka": "..." } ] } ]`
  However, in `js/app.js`:
  ```javascript
  (lesson.dialogues || []).forEach((line, idx) => {
    // accesses line.speaker, line.text_de, line.text_ka!
  });
  ```
  Since `line` is actually the dialogue container object rather than an utterance, `line.speaker` and `line.text_de` are `undefined`. In addition, `playNextDialogueLine(lesson)` attempted `speakGerman(currentLine.text_de)` on the outer dialogue object, resulting in silence or `undefined` speech synthesis.
- **Impact:**
  Dialogue tabs displayed `undefined` speaker names and text across all lessons, and sequential dialogue audio playback was completely broken.
- **Remediation:**
  Update dialogue rendering in `js/app.js` to iterate over dialogues, displaying title, context, and iterating over each dialogue's `.lines` array. Extract flat dialogue lines for the sequential player.

---

### Finding ARC-01: Offline-First Claim Contradicted by External CDN Scripts
- **File:** `index.html` (lines 10–35)
- **Evidence:**
  ```html
  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fira+GO:wght...&family=Inter:wght...&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
  ```
  While `server.py` and `js/data.js` are local, if the user disconnects from Wi-Fi or uses an offline environment, `cdn.tailwindcss.com` fails to download, rendering the app completely unstyled.
- **Remediation:**
  1. Add comprehensive offline fallback CSS in `css/style.css` ensuring essential layout (grid, flex, borders, colors, typography, buttons, badges) remains fully functional even without internet connectivity.
  2. Implement fallback system fonts (`system-ui`, `-apple-system`, `sans-serif`) in CSS.
  3. Clearly state network requirements for Google Fonts/CDN in `README.md`.

---

### Finding SEC-01: Unauthorized Goethe-Zertifikat / ÖSD Accreditation Claims
- **Files:** `index.html`, `js/app.js`, `js/data.js`, `README.md`
- **Evidence:**
  References to "Goethe / ÖSD A1 სტანდარტი", "Goethe-Zertifikat A1: Start Deutsch 1 სტანდარტი", and "VALID CERTIFICATE ✓" imply that passing this test confers an officially accredited language diploma.
- **Impact:**
  Misleading learners regarding accreditation and legal compliance.
- **Remediation:**
  - Rebrand exam: **"Deutsch A1 Pro — A1 დონის სადიაგნოსტიკო საგამოცდო სიმულატორი"** (აკადემიური მოდელირება Start Deutsch 1-ის სტრუქტურის მიხედვით).
  - Rebrand certificate: **"Deutsch A1 Pro — კურსის წარმატებით დასრულების სერტიფიკატი"**.
  - Explicitly include a legal/pedagogical disclaimer on the certificate stating that this is an internal platform progress certificate evaluating reading, listening, vocabulary, and grammar, and is not a substitute for an official Goethe-Institut or ÖSD exam.

---

### Finding SEC-02: innerHTML Interpolation Without Sanitization
- **File:** `js/app.js` (lines 923, 1037, 2276, etc.)
- **Evidence:**
  `state.fibUserInput`, lesson search strings, and dynamic text are placed into template literals inside `innerHTML`.
- **Remediation:**
  Introduce an `escapeHtml(str)` utility function in `js/app.js` and wrap all dynamic text and user inputs before template literal interpolation.

---

### Finding BIZ-01: Simulated Commercial Licensing Transparency
- **File:** `js/app.js` (lines 2110–2250), `index.html`
- **Evidence:**
  The PRO modal displays simulated pricing (₾29, ₾149) and a toggle button modifying client-side `localStorage`.
- **Remediation:**
  Explicitly designate this feature as **"PRO სასწავლო რეჟიმი (Study & Evaluation Mode)"** in the UI, transparently explaining that in this local offline edition, all advanced tools (all 734 words, Hörverstehen, Lückentext, Satzbau, Exam) are unlocked for student learning without misleading claims of bank-grade payment processing.

---

### Finding CUR-01: Imbalanced Grammar and Curriculum Depth
- **File:** `js/data.js`, `data_deep_lessons.py`
- **Evidence:**
  - L1: 4 deep sections (6,362 chars total).
  - L2: 3 sections (1,846 chars).
  - L3: 3 sections (1,985 chars).
  - L4: 1 section (925 chars) — missing furniture accusative sentences, room prepositions.
  - L5: 2 sections (871 chars) — misses colloquial 12h vs 24h clock, daily schedules.
  - L6: 1 section (513 chars) — misses leisure/hobbies, time adverbs (*oft, manchmal, nie*).
  - L7: 2 sections (689 chars) — misses orientation (*nach links, nach rechts, geradeaus*) and full Dativ prepositions.
  - L8: 2 sections (737 chars) — misses work prepositions (*als*, *bei*) and `müssen` vs `dürfen`.
  - L9: 1 section (737 chars) — misses pain expressions (*wehtun*, *Schmerzen*) and advice with `sollen`.
  - L10: 3 sections (1,312 chars) — misses comprehensive regular/irregular Perfekt list and conversational practice.
- **Remediation:**
  Expand Lessons 4 through 10 to each have 3–4 comprehensive, pedagogically sound grammar sections with Georgian contrastive analysis, real-life examples, and audio playback.

---

## 4. Remediation Plan & Execution Roadmap

- **Phase 0 (Critical Defect Fixes):**
  1. Fix `completedLessons`, `lessonScores`, and `examPassed`/`examScore` persistence.
  2. Implement timezone-aware streak tracking and XP anti-inflation.
  3. Implement `escapeHtml()` sanitization across `app.js`.
  4. Fix branding and certificate disclaimers (remove false Goethe-Institut accreditation claims).
  5. Provide offline CSS fallback rules in `css/style.css`.
  6. Transparently rebrand the PRO licensing modal.
- **Phase 1 (Curriculum & CEFR A1 Alignment):**
  1. Build a formal CEFR A1 Coverage Matrix (`CURRICULUM_MATRIX.md`).
  2. Upgrade Lessons 4 to 10 grammar, vocabulary, and contrastive notes to match the depth of Lesson 1.
- **Phase 2 (Four Core Language Labs):**
  Hören, Lesen, Schreiben, Sprechen labs implementation.
- **Phase 3 (Spaced Repetition & Retention):**
  SM-2 scheduling algorithm and mistake review.
- **Phase 4 & 5 (Interactive Branching & Diagnostic Exam):**
  Goethe-format practice modules and diagnostic scoring.
- **Phase 6 & 7 (Design Polish, Accessibility & Automated Testing):**
  Regression test suite and validation reports.

################################################################################
### FILE: server.py
################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Deutsch A1 - ლოკალური სერვერი (Localhost Server)
გერმანული ენის შემსწავლელი ქართულენოვანი პლატფორმა
"""

import http.server
import socketserver
import os
import sys
import webbrowser
import socket

# Ensure directory is German A1 root
DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
os.chdir(DIR)

DEFAULT_PORT = 8000

class CustomHTTPHandler(http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path == '/favicon.ico':
            self.send_response(204)
            self.end_headers()
            return
        super().do_GET()

    def end_headers(self):
        # Enable CORS and caching headers
        self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*')
        self.send_header('Cache-Control', 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate')
        self.send_header('Pragma', 'no-cache')
        self.send_header('Expires', '0')
        super().end_headers()

    def guess_type(self, path):
        mtype = super().guess_type(path)
        if path.endswith(".js"):
            return "application/javascript; charset=utf-8"
        if path.endswith(".css"):
            return "text/css; charset=utf-8"
        if path.endswith(".html"):
            return "text/html; charset=utf-8"
        if path.endswith(".json"):
            return "application/json; charset=utf-8"
        return mtype

    def log_message(self, format, *args):
        # Clean logging
        sys.stderr.write(f"[{self.log_date_time_string()}] {format % args}\n")

def find_available_port(start_port=DEFAULT_PORT, max_tries=20):
    for port in range(start_port, start_port + max_tries):
        with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
            if s.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) != 0:
                return port
    return DEFAULT_PORT

def main():
    port = find_available_port(DEFAULT_PORT)
    url = f"http://localhost:{port}"

    banner = f"""
======================================================================
  🇩🇪 DEUTSCH A1 • გერმანული ენის სრული პლატფორმა (A1 დონე)
  ქართულენოვანი საუნივერსიტეტო სტანდარტი (Schritte / Menschen)
======================================================================

  🌐 საიტი გაშვებულია ლოკალურ მისამართზე (Localhost):
     👉 {url}

  💡 შეგიძლიათ გახსნათ ბრაუზერში (Chrome, Safari, Firefox).
  🛑 გასაჩერებლად დააჭირეთ კლავიატურაზე: Ctrl + C
======================================================================
"""
    print(banner)

    # Automatically try opening the browser
    try:
        webbrowser.open(url)
    except Exception:
        pass

    socketserver.TCPServer.allow_reuse_address = True
    with socketserver.TCPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), CustomHTTPHandler) as httpd:
        try:
            httpd.serve_forever()
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\n🛑 სერვერი შეჩერებულია. წარმატებულ სწავლას გისურვებთ!")
            sys.exit(0)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/bin/bash
cd "$(dirname "$0")"
echo "Starting Deutsch A1 Localhost Server..."
python3 server.py

/* ==========================================================================
   DEUTSCH A1 PRO — COMPREHENSIVE PRODUCTION OFFLINE STYLESHEET
   100% Offline-First Design System with Complete Utility Fallbacks
   Bilingual Typography: Georgian (Fira GO / System) + German Umlauts (Standard)
   ========================================================================== */

:root {
  --font-main: 'Fira GO', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", "BPG Nino Mtavruli", Arial, sans-serif;

/**
 * DEUTSCH A1 PRO - ქართულ-გერმანული სასწავლო პლატფორმა
 * Modern Interactive Application Engine (Enterprise Educational Edition)
 * Includes: Core Labs (Hören, Lesen, Schreiben, Sprechen), SM-2 Spaced Repetition,
 * Robust State Persistence, Native Audio Synthesizer & Speech Recognition/MediaRecorder.
 */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  // --- Sanitization & XSS Prevention Helper ---
  function escapeHtml(str) {
    if (str === null || str === undefined) return '';
    return String(str)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
  }

  // --- Global Application State ---
  const state = {
    currentTab: 'lessons',
    selectedLessonId: 1,
    speechRate: 0.85,
    isDark: false,

    // Lexicon state
    lexiconSearch: '',

    // Article Trainer state

    trainerCurrentWord: null,

    // Sentence Builder state
    sbIndex: 0,
    sbAvailableWords: [],
    sbSelectedWords: [],
    sbChecked: false,

    // Final Exam state
    examIndex: 0,
    examUserAnswers: {},
    examSubmitted: false,
    examScore: 0,

    // Multi-Mode Lesson Exercises state
    exerciseActiveMode: 'mc', // 'mc', 'fib', 'listening'
    mcIndex: 0,
    mcSelectedAnswer: null,
    mcSubmitted: false,
    mcScore: 0,

    fibIndex: 0,
    fibUserInput: '',
    fibSubmitted: false,
    fibIsCorrect: false,
    fibScore: 0,

    lisIndex: 0,
    lisSelectedAnswer: null,
    lisSubmitted: false,

    // Dialogue Sequential Player state
    dialoguePlaying: false,

    isRecording: false,

    recordedAudioUrl: null
  };

  // --- Local Calendar Date & Streak Calculations ---
  function getLocalDateString(d = new Date()) {
    const year = d.getFullYear();
    const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
  }

  function getCalendarDaysDiff(dateStr1, dateStr2) {
    if (!dateStr1 || !dateStr2) return 0;
    const [y1, m1, d1] = dateStr1.split('-').map(Number);
    const [y2, m2, d2] = dateStr2.split('-').map(Number);
    const utc1 = Date.UTC(y1, m1 - 1, d1);
    const utc2 = Date.UTC(y2, m2 - 1, d2);
    return Math.round((utc2 - utc1) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  }

  function updateDailyStreak(data) {
    const todayStr = getLocalDateString();
    if (!data.lastActiveDate) {
      data.lastActiveDate = todayStr;
      data.streak = 1;
      return;
    }
    if (data.lastActiveDate === todayStr) {
      return; // Already recorded activity today
    }
    const diffDays = getCalendarDaysDiff(data.lastActiveDate, todayStr);
    if (diffDays === 1) {
      data.streak = (data.streak || 0) + 1;
      data.lastActiveDate = todayStr;
    } else if (diffDays > 1) {
      data.streak = 1;
      data.lastActiveDate = todayStr;
    }
  }

  function getCleanDefaultUserData() {
    return {
      xp: 0,
      streak: 1,
      lastActiveDate: getLocalDateString(),
      completedLessons: [],
      lessonScores: {},
      lessonSkillProgress: {}, // lessonId -> { grammarReviewed, vocabPracticed, mcScore, mcPassed, fibScore, fibPassed, lisScore, lisPassed }
      masteredWords: [],
      favoriteWords: [],
      srsItems: {}, // wordId -> { interval, repetition, efactor, nextReviewDate }
      mistakeWords: [],
      claimedRewards: {}, // rewardKey -> timestamp
      labScores: { hoeren: {}, lesen: {}, schreiben: {}, sprechen: {} },
      examHistory: [], // [{ attempt, date, score, total, pct, passed }]
      examPassed: false,
      examScore: null,
      examDate: null,
      isPro: true,
      subscriptionTier: 'pro_annual'
    };
  }

  // --- Local Storage Management & State Persistence (Stage 8) ---
  const STORAGE_KEY = 'deutsch_a1_pro_v3';
  const RESET_SENTINEL = 'deutsch_a1_reset_marker_v3';

  let userData = loadUserData();

  function loadUserData() {
    try {
      const wasReset = localStorage.getItem(RESET_SENTINEL);
      let raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);

      // Only migrate legacy if user never explicitly clicked "Reset All Progress"
      if (!raw && !wasReset) {
        const v2 = localStorage.getItem('deutsch_a1_pro_v2') || localStorage.getItem('deutsch_a1_user_data_v1');
        if (v2) {
          raw = v2;
          localStorage.removeItem('deutsch_a1_pro_v2');
          localStorage.removeItem('deutsch_a1_user_data_v1');
        }
      }

      let data = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
      if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
        data = getCleanDefaultUserData();
      } else {
        // Enforce strict schema validation & defaults
        data.xp = (typeof data.xp === 'number' && !isNaN(data.xp)) ? Math.max(0, data.xp) : 0;
        data.streak = (typeof data.streak === 'number' && !isNaN(data.streak)) ? Math.max(1, data.streak) : 1;
        data.completedLessons = Array.isArray(data.completedLessons) ? data.completedLessons.filter(n => typeof n === 'number') : [];
        data.lessonScores = (typeof data.lessonScores === 'object' && data.lessonScores !== null) ? data.lessonScores : {};
        data.lessonSkillProgress = (typeof data.lessonSkillProgress === 'object' && data.lessonSkillProgress !== null) ? data.lessonSkillProgress : {};
        data.masteredWords = Array.isArray(data.masteredWords) ? data.masteredWords : [];
        data.favoriteWords = Array.isArray(data.favoriteWords) ? data.favoriteWords : [];
        data.srsItems = (typeof data.srsItems === 'object' && data.srsItems !== null) ? data.srsItems : {};
        data.mistakeWords = Array.isArray(data.mistakeWords) ? data.mistakeWords : [];
        data.claimedRewards = (typeof data.claimedRewards === 'object' && data.claimedRewards !== null) ? data.claimedRewards : {};
        data.labScores = (typeof data.labScores === 'object' && data.labScores !== null) ? data.labScores : { hoeren: {}, lesen: {}, schreiben: {}, sprechen: {} };
        data.examHistory = Array.isArray(data.examHistory) ? data.examHistory : [];
        data.examPassed = Boolean(data.examPassed);
        data.examScore = typeof data.examScore === 'number' ? data.examScore : null;
        data.examDate = typeof data.examDate === 'string' ? data.examDate : null;
        data.isPro = true;

        updateDailyStreak(data);
      }
      return data;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to load user data, restoring safe defaults:', e);
      return getCleanDefaultUserData();
    }
  }

  function saveUserData() {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(userData));
      updateHeaderStats();
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to save user data:', e);
    }
  }

  // --- Idempotent Reward Management (Fixes Priority 0 Defect 1: XP Exploits) ---
  function grantRewardXP(rewardKey, amount, successMessage) {
    userData.claimedRewards = userData.claimedRewards || {};

    if (userData.claimedRewards[rewardKey]) {
      // Reward already claimed: do not grant duplicate XP
      if (successMessage) {
        showToast(successMessage + ' (XP უკვე მიღებულია)');
      }
      saveUserData();
      return false;
    }

    userData.claimedRewards[rewardKey] = new Date().toISOString();
    userData.xp = (userData.xp || 0) + amount;
    saveUserData();
    playChime('success');
    showToast((successMessage ? successMessage + ' ' : '') + `+${amount} XP! ⭐`);
    updateHeaderStats();
    return true;
  }

  function addXP(amount) {
    userData.xp = (userData.xp || 0) + amount;
    saveUserData();
    playChime('success');
    showToast(`+${amount} XP! ⭐`);
  }

  // --- Multi-Skill Lesson Mastery Tracker (Fixes Priority 0 Defect 3) ---
  function recordSkillProgress(lessonId, skillKey, scorePct, passed) {
    userData.lessonSkillProgress = userData.lessonSkillProgress || {};
    userData.lessonScores = userData.lessonScores || {};
    
    let p = userData.lessonSkillProgress[lessonId] || {
      grammarReviewed: false,
      vocabPracticed: false,
      mcScore: 0,
      mcPassed: false,
      fibScore: 0,
      fibPassed: false,
      lisScore: 0,
      lisPassed: false
    };

    if (skillKey === 'grammar') {
      p.grammarReviewed = true;
    } else if (skillKey === 'vocab') {
      p.vocabPracticed = true;
    } else if (skillKey === 'mc') {
      p.mcScore = Math.max(p.mcScore || 0, scorePct);
      if (passed) p.mcPassed = true;
    } else if (skillKey === 'fib') {
      p.fibScore = Math.max(p.fibScore || 0, scorePct);
      if (passed) p.fibPassed = true;
    } else if (skillKey === 'lis') {
      p.lisScore = Math.max(p.lisScore || 0, scorePct);
      if (passed) p.lisPassed = true;
    }

    userData.lessonSkillProgress[lessonId] = p;

    // Overall composite lesson score (average of passed skills)
    const testedSkills = [p.mcScore, p.fibScore, p.lisScore].filter(s => s > 0);
    const avgScore = testedSkills.length ? Math.round(testedSkills.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / testedSkills.length) : 0;
    if (avgScore > (userData.lessonScores[lessonId] || 0)) {
      userData.lessonScores[lessonId] = avgScore;
    }

    checkAndAwardLessonMastery(lessonId);
  }

  function checkAndAwardLessonMastery(lessonId) {
    userData.completedLessons = userData.completedLessons || [];
    const p = (userData.lessonSkillProgress && userData.lessonSkillProgress[lessonId]) || {};
    
    // Strict pedagogical criteria: Grammar reviewed, Vocab trained, and MC, FIB, Listening passed (>= 70%)
    const isMastered = Boolean(
      p.grammarReviewed &&
      p.vocabPracticed &&
      p.mcPassed &&
      p.fibPassed &&
      p.lisPassed
    );

    if (isMastered && !userData.completedLessons.includes(lessonId)) {
      userData.completedLessons.push(lessonId);
      userData.completedLessons.sort((a, b) => a - b);
      grantRewardXP('lesson_mastery_' + lessonId, 50, `გილოცავთ! გაკვეთილი ${lessonId} სრულად ათვისებულია (5-ვე უნარი)! 🎓`);
      playChime('fanfare');
    } else {
      saveUserData();
    }
    updateHeaderStats();
  }

  // --- SM-2 Spaced Repetition Algorithm (SuperMemo-2) ---
  function updateSM2Item(wordId, quality) {
    userData.srsItems = userData.srsItems || {};
    let item = userData.srsItems[wordId] || {
      interval: 1,
      repetition: 0,
      efactor: 2.5,
      nextReviewDate: getLocalDateString()
    };

    if (quality >= 3) {
      if (item.repetition === 0) {
        item.interval = 1;
      } else if (item.repetition === 1) {
        item.interval = 6;
      } else {
        item.interval = Math.round(item.interval * item.efactor);
      }
      item.repetition += 1;
      // Remove from mistakes if successfully remembered
      if (userData.mistakeWords) {
        userData.mistakeWords = userData.mistakeWords.filter(id => id !== wordId);
      }
    } else {
      item.repetition = 0;
      item.interval = 1;
      // Add to mistakes queue
      userData.mistakeWords = userData.mistakeWords || [];
      if (!userData.mistakeWords.includes(wordId)) {
        userData.mistakeWords.push(wordId);
      }
    }

    // Calculate next ease factor
    item.efactor = item.efactor + (0.1 - (5 - quality) * (0.08 + (5 - quality) * 0.02));
    if (item.efactor < 1.3) item.efactor = 1.3;

    // Calculate next local review date
    const nextDate = new Date();
    nextDate.setDate(nextDate.getDate() + item.interval);
    item.nextReviewDate = getLocalDateString(nextDate);

    userData.srsItems[wordId] = item;
    saveUserData();
  }

  // --- Web Audio API Chimes (Pure Native Acoustics) ---
  function playChime(type) {
    try {
      const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AudioContext) return;
      const ctx = new AudioContext();

      if (type === 'success') {
        const osc1 = ctx.createOscillator();
        const osc2 = ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = ctx.createGain();

        osc1.type = 'sine';
        osc2.type = 'sine';
        osc1.frequency.setValueAtTime(523.25, ctx.currentTime); // C5
        osc2.frequency.setValueAtTime(659.25, ctx.currentTime + 0.1); // E5

        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, ctx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.35);

        osc1.connect(gain);
        osc2.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(ctx.destination);

        osc1.start(ctx.currentTime);
        osc1.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.12);
        osc2.start(ctx.currentTime + 0.1);
        osc2.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.35);
      } else if (type === 'wrong') {
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = ctx.createGain();

        osc.type = 'triangle';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(260, ctx.currentTime);
        osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(180, ctx.currentTime + 0.25);

        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, ctx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.3);

        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(ctx.destination);

        osc.start(ctx.currentTime);
        osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.3);
      } else if (type === 'fanfare') {
        const notes = [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5]; // C5, E5, G5, C6
        notes.forEach((freq, i) => {
          const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
          const gain = ctx.createGain();
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, ctx.currentTime + i * 0.1);
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, ctx.currentTime + i * 0.1);
          gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + i * 0.1 + 0.35);
          osc.connect(gain);
          gain.connect(ctx.destination);
          osc.start(ctx.currentTime + i * 0.1);
          osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + i * 0.1 + 0.35);
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // AudioContext blocked or silent error
    }
  }

  // --- Native Web Speech Audio Engine ---
  function speakGerman(text, buttonElement, onEndCallback) {
    if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
      showToast('ხმის გაჟღერების მხარდაჭერა არ არის ბრაუზერში');
      if (onEndCallback) onEndCallback();
      return;
    }
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    const cleanText = text
      .replace(/[()]/g, '')
      .replace(/,\s*-[a-zäöüß]+/gi, '')
      .replace(/•/g, '')
      .trim();

    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(cleanText);
    utterance.lang = 'de-DE';
    utterance.rate = state.speechRate;

    const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
    const deVoice = voices.find(v => v.lang.startsWith('de') || v.lang.includes('DE'));
    if (deVoice) utterance.voice = deVoice;

    if (buttonElement) {
      buttonElement.classList.add('speaking-active');
      utterance.onend = () => {
        buttonElement.classList.remove('speaking-active');
        if (onEndCallback) onEndCallback();
      };
      utterance.onerror = () => {
        buttonElement.classList.remove('speaking-active');
        if (onEndCallback) onEndCallback();
      };
    } else {
      utterance.onend = () => {
        if (onEndCallback) onEndCallback();
      };
      utterance.onerror = () => {
        if (onEndCallback) onEndCallback();
      };
    }

    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  }

  // --- Toast Notifications ---
  function showToast(message) {
    let container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
    if (!container) {
      container = document.createElement('div');
      container.id = 'toast-container';
      container.className = 'fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-50 flex flex-col gap-2 pointer-events-none';
      document.body.appendChild(container);
    }
    const toast = document.createElement('div');
    toast.className = 'bg-slate-900 text-white dark:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl shadow-xl text-sm font-medium animate-fadeIn flex items-center gap-2 pointer-events-auto border border-slate-700 dark:border-slate-300';
    toast.innerHTML = message;
    container.appendChild(toast);
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'transition-opacity', 'duration-300');
      setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
    }, 2500);
  }

  // --- Tab Navigation Engine ---
  function setTab(tabName) {
    state.currentTab = tabName;

    // Stop sequential dialogue if running
    stopSequentialDialogue();

    // Update active state in nav buttons
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-tab').forEach(btn => {
      if (btn.dataset.tab === tabName) {
        btn.classList.add('active');
        btn.classList.remove('text-slate-600', 'dark:text-slate-300');
      } else {
        btn.classList.remove('active');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-600', 'dark:text-slate-300');
      }
    });

    const content = document.getElementById('main-content');
    if (!content) return;
    content.innerHTML = '';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });

    switch (tabName) {
      case 'lessons':
        renderLessonsList(content);
        break;
      case 'lesson-detail':
        renderLessonDetail(content, state.selectedLessonId);
        break;
      case 'lexicon':
        renderLexicon(content);
        break;
      case 'flashcards':
        initFlashcards();
        renderFlashcards(content);
        break;
      case 'article-trainer':
        initArticleTrainer();
        renderArticleTrainer(content);
        break;
      case 'sentence-builder':
        initSentenceBuilder();
        renderSentenceBuilder(content);
        break;
      case 'labs':
        renderLanguageLabs(content);
        break;
      case 'grammar':
        renderGrammarHandbook(content);
        break;
      case 'exam':
        renderExam(content);
        break;
      case 'profile':
        renderProfile(content);
        break;
      default:
        renderLessonsList(content);
    }
  }

  function updateHeaderStats() {
    const elStreak = document.getElementById('stat-streak');
    const elXP = document.getElementById('stat-xp');
    const elBar = document.getElementById('stat-progress-bar');
    const elText = document.getElementById('stat-progress-text');

    if (elStreak) elStreak.textContent = userData.streak || 1;
    if (elXP) elXP.textContent = userData.xp || 0;

    // Progress = (completedLessons / 10) * 70% + (masteredWords / 734) * 30%
    const compLessons = (userData.completedLessons || []).length;
    const mastWords = (userData.masteredWords || []).length;
    const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((compLessons / 10) * 70 + (mastWords / 734) * 30));

    if (elBar) elBar.style.width = `${pct}%`;
    if (elText) elText.textContent = `${pct}%`;
  }

              <h2 class="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">A1 სასწავლო მოდულები</h2>

            </div>
            <span class="text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 rounded-full">

          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
    `;

    lessons.forEach(l => {
      const isCompleted = (userData.completedLessons || []).includes(l.id);
      const score = (userData.lessonScores || {})[l.id];
      const ex = l.exercises || {};
      const mcCount = (ex.multiple_choice || []).length;
      const fibCount = (ex.fill_in_blank || []).length;
      const lisCount = (ex.listening || []).length;
      const totalTests = mcCount + fibCount + lisCount;

      html += `
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-3xl p-6 hover:shadow-xl hover:border-blue-500/40 transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col justify-between group">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between mb-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                <span class="text-4xl p-3 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-slate-700/60 rounded-2xl group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">${l.icon}</span>
                <div>
                  <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span class="text-xs font-black text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 tracking-wider uppercase">LEKTION ${l.id}</span>
                    <span class="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">${l.level || 'A1'}</span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-snug">${escapeHtml(l.title_ka)}</h3>
                </div>
              </div>
              ${isCompleted ? `
                <span class="bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 text-xs font-extrabold px-3 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
                  ✓ ჩაბარებულია (${score || 100}%)
                </span>
              ` : `
                <span class="bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                  გასავლელია
                </span>
              `}
            </div>

            <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300 italic mb-2">${escapeHtml(l.title_de)}</p>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-5 leading-relaxed font-normal">${escapeHtml(l.description_ka || '')}</p>

            ${l.objectives_ka && l.objectives_ka.length ? `
              <div class="p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700/30 rounded-xl mb-4 text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 space-y-1">
                <span class="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 block">🎯 ძირითადი თემები:</span>
                <p class="line-clamp-2">• ${l.objectives_ka.slice(0, 3).map(escapeHtml).join(' • ')}</p>
              </div>
            ` : ''}
          </div>

          <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/60">
            <div class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-2">
              <span>📚 ${l.grammar_sections ? l.grammar_sections.length : 0} თეორია</span>
              <span>•</span>
              <span>💬 ${l.dialogues ? l.dialogues.length : 0} დიალოგი</span>
              <span>•</span>
              <span class="text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400">✍️ ${totalTests} ტესტი</span>
            </div>
            <button onclick="window.DeutschApp.openLesson(${l.id})" class="text-xs font-extrabold bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-all group-hover:translate-x-0.5 flex items-center gap-1">
              <span>გახსნა</span>
              <span>→</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    container.innerHTML = html;
  }

  // ==========================================================================
  // TAB 1.1: DEEP LESSON DETAIL VIEW
  // ==========================================================================
  function renderLessonDetail(container, lessonId) {
